www.lhratings.com  研究报告 1

内需亟待破局——10 月经济数据点评 
联合资信  主权部 
2026 年 10 月，宏观经济延续“外强内弱、新强旧弱”格局，出口韧性超预期构成核心支
撑，但内需偏弱矛盾进一步显性化。外贸端，10 月出口同比增长 35.2%，机电产品占比达 67.4%，
集成电路、自动数据处理设备等 AI 产业链产品高速增长，但集成电路等品类呈现“量减价升”，
价格因素对名义增速贡献显著上升；进口同比增长 41.6%，同样受 AI 产业链与能源价格推动。
投资端压力加剧，1—10 月固定资产投资同比下降 8.1%，房地产开发投资下降 21.6%，基建与
制造业投资同步走弱，仅知识产权产品投资（+9.2%）及高技术产业投资（+4.0%）维持韧性。
消费复苏动能衰减，10 月社零同比仅增 0.4%，服务零售额增速（4.9%）显著快于商品零售
（0.9%），汽车类消费构成主要拖累。价格端，CPI 同比上涨 0.7%、PPI 同比上涨 5.7%，剪刀
差扩大至 5 个百分点，反映输入性成本压力与内生需求偏弱的并存。总体看，新质生产力相关
领域仍是结构性亮点，但体量尚不足以完全对冲传统板块下行压力，内需修复仍需政策进一步
发力。

### Page 2

www.lhratings.com  研究报告 1
2026 年 10 月宏观经济运行延续了“外强内弱、新强旧弱”的核心格局，但内需
偏弱的矛盾进一步显性化，且价格端与需求端的分化呈现出新的特征。出口超预期韧
性仍是最大支撑，工业新动能持续高增，然而固定资产投资降幅加深、消费复苏动能
衰减、CPI 与 PPI 剪刀差走阔，共同指向经济修复的内生动力依然不足，政策托底的
必要性进一步上升。
一、进出口表现依旧强劲，价格因素贡献上升
2026 年 10 月出口表现强劲，增速虽较 9 月历史高位略有回落，但仍远超市场预
期，出口绝对额创历史同期新高。以美元计价，10 月出口 4123.3 亿美元，同比增长
35.2%，较上月微降 1.1 个百分点，已连续七个月保持两位数增长。2026 年 10 月进口
表现同样强劲，增速甚至超过出口，10 月进口 3070.5 亿美元，同比增长 41.6%，较 9
月（31.0%）大幅提升 10.6 个百分点。尽管出口额增速低于进口额，但出口数量增速
仍高于进口数量增速，价格因素对进口名义高增的贡献比出口更为突出，实际贸易顺

差增长好于名义表现。
AI 产业链是核心引擎，但价格因素贡献上升。出口高增的首要支撑来自全球 AI
相关产业的高景气。机电产品 10 月出口 2779.82 亿美元，占出口总额比重约 67.4%，
同比增长 46.1%，增速较上月进一步上行。其中，集成电路出口同比飙升 137.1%，自
动数据处理设备及其零部件同比增长 114.8%，两者是机电品类中增速最快的分项。
高新技术产品整体出口增长 66.6%，同样维持高位。
但值得注意的是，部分品类的高增更多由价格因素而非数量扩张驱动。以集成电
路为例，10 月其出口数量同比由正转负（从 0.9%降至-2.5%），而出口价格同比从 133.8%
进一步上行至 143.2%。这意味着 AI 相关商品的价格上涨对名义出口增速形成了显著
推升，实际出口量的增长力度可能弱于表观读数。
与此同时，传统劳动密集型产品出口增速明显回落。家具玩具、服装箱包等品类
10 月增速较上月走弱，出口增长的动力重新向高端制造集中，结构扩散效应有所减
弱。
AI 相关产品是进口增长的第一引擎。10 月自动数据处理设备及其零部件进口同

比增长 261%，集成电路进口增长 78.3%，两者合计拉动进口增长约 21.7 个百分点，
贡献了超过一半的进口同比增量。这与出口端集成电路、自动数据处理设备的高增形
成呼应，反映出全球 AI 产业链的贸易流在中国进出口两端同时放量。能源类商品同
样呈现“量减价升”特征。10 月原油进口金额同比增长约 41%，但进口数量同比下
降 13.3%；煤炭进口金额增长 60%，数量同比下降 5.5%。国际油价中枢抬升直接推
高了进口支出，而国内实物需求的恢复力度从这个数据看并不强劲。

### Page 3

www.lhratings.com  研究报告 2 
东盟稳居第一，对欧创新高，对美受低基数支撑。分贸易伙伴看，东盟仍是第一
大出口市场，10 月对东盟出口 779.75 亿美元，同比增长 46.3%，虽较 9 月的 52.3%
小幅放缓，但仍显著高于整体增速，占出口总额的 18.9%。基建需求、补库周期及产
业链转移带动的中间品贸易是核心拉动力。东盟仍是最大进口来源地，10 月自东盟
进口 461.0 亿美元，1—10 月累计增长 28.1%。自越南进口增长尤为突出（当月同比
约 55%），反映产业链转移背景下中间品贸易的深化。自美国进口当月约 137.1 亿美
元，累计增长 7.8%，增速相对温和。

二、固定资产投资格局深化，新动能是稳定增长项 
2026 年 1—10 月全国固定资产投资降幅进一步扩大，是拖累内需的主要因素。
国家统计局数据显示，1—10 月全国固定资产投资（不含农户）同比下降 8.1%，降幅
较前三季度（-7.2%）继续加深，且显著弱于市场预期（预期为-7%）。从当月同比看，
10 月当月固定资产投资下降 16.76%，显示投资动能仍在收缩。 
三大领域投资全面走弱格局进一步深化，新动能成为固定资产投资中稳定增长
项。房地产开发投资是最大拖累项，1—10 月同比下降 21.6%，降幅较前三季度进一
步扩大。新建商品房销售面积同比下降 13.7%，销售额下降 14.3%，销售端未见企稳
迹象。基础设施投资同比下降 5.1%，同样较前三季度的-4.2%走弱。尽管信息传输业
投资增长 32.0%、航空运输业增长 12.5%，但水利、公共设施管理等传统基建板块降
幅较大（水利管理业-16.7%，公共设施管理业-14.1%），对整体基建形成拖累。制造业
投资同比下降 2.9%，较前三季度（-2.1%）降幅扩大。 
分行业看，铁路船舶航空航天等运输设备制造业投资增长 14.1%，是少数亮点；
但汽车制造业（-7.9%）、专用设备制造业（-9.0%）、医药制造业（-9.2%）等板块收缩
明显，反映传统制造领域扩产意愿偏弱。在整体投资下行背景下，部分新兴领域投资

保持增长。知识产权产品投资同比增长 9.2%，是投资中少有的稳定增长项。高技术
产业投资同比增长 4.0%，其中信息服务业增长 20.5%、航空航天器及设备制造业增长
10.2%、电子及通信设备制造业增长 6.6%。设备工器具购置投资同比增长 8.9%，反映
大规模设备更新政策仍在发挥一定效果。 
分地区看，东北地区投资下降 25.9%，中部（-12.9%）、东部（-11.5%）、西部（-
9.2%）均呈两位数或接近两位数的降幅，区域分化中东北压力尤为突出。民间投资同
比下降 11.4%，降幅大于整体投资，扣除房地产后仍下降 7.8%，显示民营企业投资信
心依然不足。 
总体来看，10 月固定资产投资延续并加剧了此前的下行趋势，房地产深度调整、
基建实物工作量形成偏慢、制造业扩产意愿不足压力叠加。然而，新质生产力相关投
资持续发力，一定程度对冲传统投资下行压力，成为固定资产投资中唯一的稳定增长
项。

三、消费内部分化显著，服务消费强、商品消费偏弱 
2026 年 10 月消费数据表现疲弱，增速进一步回落。国家统计局数据显示，1—
10 月社会消费品零售总额累计同比增长 1.0%。10 月，社会消费品零售总额 46463 亿
元，同比仅增长 0.4%，较 9 月回落 0.7 个百分点，环比增长 0.02%。 
服务消费仍强于商品，汽车是主要拖累项。2026 年 1—10 月服务零售额同比增
长 4.9%，比商品零售额增速（0.9%）快 4 个百分点。旅游咨询租赁和文体休闲等服
务品类增速明显快于整体服务零售。10 月，商品零售同比仅增 0.3%，餐饮收入增长
0.7%，增速回落主要受中秋错月（去年中秋在 10 月、今年在 9 月，部分消费提前释
放）以及部分耐用消费品销售放缓影响。分行业看，除汽车以外的消费品零售额同比
增长 2.5%，而整体社零增速仅 0.4%，汽车类消费对整体消费形成了显著拖累。不过，

限额以上单位通讯器材类、文化办公用品类 10 月同比分别增长 6.5%和 4.1%，部分
升级类商品保持较快增长。从前 10 个月累计看，通讯器材类增长 16.5%，可穿戴智
能设备零售额增速超过 70%。 
总体来看，10 月消费延续了服务消费仍强于商品的分化格局，汽车拖累显著，政
策托底品类（通讯器材等）尚有支撑，但整体消费动能较前三季度进一步走弱，居民
预防性储蓄倾向和消费信心修复偏慢的问题未见明显改善。

四、上游价格高企、下游需求疲弱 
10 月 CPI 同比上涨 0.7%，涨幅较上月回落 0.2 个百分点；PPI 同比上涨 5.7%，
涨幅较上月扩大 0.7 个百分点。PPI 与 CPI 剪刀差扩大至 5 个百分点。 
从 CPI 端看，食品价格同比下降 2.9%是主要拖累，鲜菜、鸡蛋等价格大幅下跌
抵消了汽油价格上涨的影响。但更值得关注的是核心 CPI 同比仅上涨 0.8%，涨幅同
样回落 0.2 个百分点，表明剔除食品和能源后的内生性价格压力依然偏弱。从 PPI 端
看，涨幅扩大主要集中在上游能源相关行业，汽油价格同比上涨 22.4%影响 CPI 同比
上涨约 0.63 个百分点，地缘政治驱动的输入性成本压力是 PPI 上行的主因，而非国
内工业品需求的实质性改善。中下游企业面临原材料成本上升而终端售价难以同步传
导的困境，利润空间受到挤压，这在一定程度上解释了制造业投资意愿偏弱和传统工
业板块收缩的持续性。 
总体来看，10 月出口的韧性虽然超预期，但其对经济增长的净拉动在价格因素
和贸易条件变化下可能被高估，投资和消费的双重疲弱使内需成为稳增长的主要短板，
而政策工具的传导时滞意味着短期内的企稳仍需耐心。新质生产力的强劲扩张是当前
经济中最确定的结构性亮点，但其体量尚不足以完全对冲传统板块的下行压力。在外
部地缘风险与贸易博弈持续扰动的环境下，以自身发展的确定性对冲外部不确定性，
需要政策在扩大内需上展现出更强的力度和更精准的指向。

115年 10月賦稅收入初步統計 
一、整體賦稅收入 
（一）實徵淨額 
10 月實徵淨額 5,669 億元，較上年同月增加 1,495 億元；累計 1 至
10 月實徵 4 兆 1,383 億元，較上年同期增加 8,499 億元(+25.8%)，
主要由證交稅、營所稅及營業稅所貢獻。隨新版財政收支劃分法實
施，營業稅劃歸中央政府比率由 61.2%降為 4.5%(獲配稅額隨之減
少 2,662 億元)，致中央政府稅收增加數 3,906 億元(+15.8%)，不及
地方政府增加數 4,671 億元(+68.5%)。 
（二）預算達成率 
累 計1 至 10 月 實 徵 淨 額 占 全 年 預 算 數 91.4% ， 其 中 中 央 政 府 為
92.8%，地方政府為87.7%；與全年預算數差距分別為3,913億元、
2,227億元、1,614億元。 
圖 1 賦稅實徵淨額及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至 6 月底所致。

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）10 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營利事業所得稅增加 1,133 億元、
證券交易稅增加 220 億元及綜合所得稅增加 120 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,297
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 2,986 億元及營業稅增加 1,272 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-10 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)10 月實徵淨額 4,144 億元，較上年同月增加 1,133 億元(+37.6%)，
主因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 1 兆 4,172 億元，較上年同期增加 2,986
億元(+26.7%)，主因結算申報自繳、暫繳稅款俱增及延分期稅款
陸續入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
10 月實徵淨額 614 億元，較上年同月增加 120 億元(+24.3%)，主因
扣繳稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 8,894 億元，較上年同
期增加 910 億元(+11.4%)，主因結算申報自繳稅額增加，以及盈餘分

Document 033: 2026-11-13_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］160円台はやはり「円安すぎ」ではない_b6d02f4b

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-13_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］160円台はやはり「円安すぎ」ではない_b6d02f4b.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

160 円台はやはり「円安すぎ」ではない 
～164 円への円安も視野、介入警戒の磁力が一段安を阻むか～

調査部  主任エコノミスト 東深澤 武史 
080-1069-4919  takeshi.higashifukasawa@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ ドル円の安定はかりそめ 
9月下旬以降、1ドル＝150円台後半での小動きが続いたが、これをもって「相場が安定している」と
捉えるのは危うい。チャート上は、上値目途として意識される200日移動平均線が150円台半ばに位置
し、同水準へ向けた円安方向への動きが繰り返された（図表1）。このところの値動きの狭さは、円安
方向を根強く試す動きが抵抗線付近で抑えられた結果とみる方が実態に近そうだ。 
11月に入ってからは、円高になりうる要因が散見されたにもかかわらず、市場ではあまり材料視さ
れていない。米国中間選挙（11月3日）の投開票後には、米国のデータセンター建設投資の持続性に対
する懸念が強まったことで株価が大きく下落した（図表2）。震源地である米国から日本に資金を退避
する動きが生じれば円高要因になるが、ドル円の値動きは限定的だった。また、中東情勢に対する楽
観的な見方から原油安が進展しており、円需給の観点からは先行きの本邦輸入金額の減少を通じて円
安圧力を緩和する要因になるが、この点も現時点では円高につながっていない。 
足元では、米利上げ観測の再燃を背景に再び円安が進んでおり、円安圧力は一段と強まっている。
本稿執筆時点では、心理的節目の200日移動平均線を超え、159円台後半まで上昇している。 
図表1 ドル円レート 図表2 日米株価指数

### Page 2

2

本稿では、円安圧力の背景にある要因を改めて整理したうえで、それらを考慮したドル円の推計値
を試算する。実勢値との比較を通じて、足元の円安水準が現在の経済環境に照らしてどの程度説明で
きるのか検証し、その結果をもとに先行きのドル円を展望する。 
■ 現時点でのドル円の適正水準は 1 ドル＝163.70 円 
1ドル＝160円の円安を試す動きは、現在の経済環境に裏付けられたものなのだろうか。この点を検
証するために、東深澤（2026）で構築したモデルを用いて、現在の経済環境から説明されるドル円の
水準を確認する（推計式の詳細や構築過程については東深澤（2026）を参照されたい）。このモデル
を用いて足元の経済環境と整合的なドル円を試算すると、推計値は1ドル＝163.70円と実勢値より円安
になる（図表3）。足元の経済環境下では、160円台はやはり「円安すぎ」ではないということだ。 
モデルが円安水準を支持している理由として最も大きいのが、日本の期待インフレ率の上昇だ。2022
年以降の円安を要因分解すると、米金利上昇や実需の円需給環境の悪化に加え、日本の期待インフレ
率の上昇が大きく寄与している（図表4）。物価が上がれば、同じ金額で買える財・サービスが少なく
なり、円の購買力は低下する。先行きも物価上昇が続くとの見方が強まれば（＝期待インフレ率の上
昇）、将来の円の価値が低下することを織り込む過程で、現在の円相場にも円安圧力がかかると考え
られる。 
実際、日本の期待インフレ率上昇と円安との関係は、データからも確認される。図表5をみると、日
米期待インフレ率格差が縮小すると、円安が進むという対応関係がうかがえる。米国の期待インフレ
率はおおむね安定しており1、日米期待インフレ率格差の縮小は、主に日本の期待インフレ率が上昇し
ていることによる。こうした期待インフレ率の高まりは、賃金・物価上昇の定着にともなう構造的な
変化2だ。期待インフレ率が反転して大幅に低下することは短期的に見込みづらいため、1ドル＝160円
程度の円安水準が定着することは自然だと言える。 
加えて、足元では米金利の上昇も円安圧力となっているようだ。11月以降は、市場予想を上回る物
図表3 ドル円の推計値と実勢値 図表4 2022年以降の円安要因

### Page 3

3

価指標や、ウォラー理事など複数のFRB高官発言がタカ派的と受け止められたことを背景に、米金利
は再度上昇に転じつつある。 
■ 今後は、介入警戒の磁力が一段の円安を阻むかが焦点に 
先行き、推計値（1ドル=163.70円）との乖離を解消する形で円安が進む可能性は十分にある。円安進
行のドライバーになりうるのが、投機筋に積み上がった円買いポジションの巻き戻しだ。図表6は、実
勢値と推計値の乖離を、投機筋の円ポジションに重ねたものだ。これをみると、円買いポジションが
拡大する局面では、実勢値が推計値より円高になる場面が多いことがわかる。とりわけ9月中旬にかけ
ては、日銀の追加利上げ観測などを背景に投機筋の円買い超過幅が拡大した。円買いポジション調整
の余地は十分にあり、巻き戻しの動きが顕在化する場合は1ドル＝164円近傍までの円安進行が視野に
入ってくるということだ。 
こうしたシナリオが実現するかどうかは、為替介入への警戒の持続性に左右される。7月末の日米協
調介入も記憶に新しく、今のところ為替市場は介入への警戒感を維持していると考えられる。11月12
日には三村財務官が円安をけん制する発言を行うなど、日本政府が再び介入姿勢を強めている点を踏
まえれば、160円を持続的に上回るような円安進行は回避されるという見方が穏当だ。 
ただし、リスクは円安方向に大きい。特に、協調介入に対する米国側の姿勢が反転するシナリオに
注意しておきたい。7月当時と比べると、米国政府にとって協調介入の必要性が低下している可能性が
あるためだ。米国政府は当時、日本の長期金利上昇と円安が米国債市場に波及し、米金利を押し上げ
る経路を意識していたとみられる。足元では日米ともに長期金利の上昇が一服しており、日本発の市
場の混乱を防ぐ動機が弱まっている可能性は相応にある。今後、米国政府高官の発言などを機に、米
国が協調介入実施に慎重という見方が広がれば、市場の介入警戒感が和らぎかねない。その場合は、
投機筋のポジション調整を伴う形で、160円を上回って円安が進行するリスクに注意が必要だ。 
以上を踏まえ、2026年末にかけては、米利上げ観測が続き、150円台後半～160円近辺での円安水準
が続く展開を予想する。ただし、投機筋の円買いが巻き戻されたり、協調介入への警戒が和らいだり
図表5 ドル円と日米期待インフレ格差 図表6 推計値と実績値の差及び投機筋の動向

すれば、164円近辺までの円安は十分に射程圏内に入る。為替介入が実施されれば1ドル＝150円程度ま
での一時的な円高は想定されるものの、その後は再び円安を試す動きが強まりやすいだろう。

［参考文献］ 
東深澤武史（2026）「160円台が「円安すぎ」ではない理由～「期待インフレ上昇」という新たな構造
変化」、みずほ総合研究所『みずほ経済 EXPRESS』、2026年7月29日 
東深澤武史ほか（2024）「円安が進むドル円の再評価～緩やかな円高予想も、日米金利や円需給を巡る
不確実性は円安リスクに」、みずほリサーチ＆テクノロジーズ『みずほリポート』、2024年4
月23日

1 この間、米国の期待インフレ率は概ね 2%台半ばで横ばい推移となっている。2026 年の中東情勢悪化を受けて急速な原油高が
進展した場面でも、期待インフレ率の急激な上昇は回避されている。背景には、米国の利上げ期待が急速に進んだことにある。
FRB が迅速に利上げを進めることで、先行きのインフレ率は安定するだろうという市場の期待が反映されていると解釈すること
ができる。 
2 実需要因も、円相場の趨勢を考えるうえで重要な要因である。足元の相場変動への寄与は限定的なため本文では詳述していな
いが、実需面の構造変化に関する分析は東深澤ほか（2024）を参照されたい。

Document 074: 2026年11月16日投資研究週報

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年11月16日投資研究週報.pdf
Report source: Mega Bank (投資研究週報)
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 17
Status: ok

### Page 1

兆豐銀行所做任何投資意見與市場分析結果，係依據資料製作當時情況進行判斷
惟可能因市場變化而變動，投資標的之價格與收益將隨時變動，亦不必然為未來績效表現 1

市場焦點
美國
• 10月CPI月增0.4%，較前月放緩，但核心CPI連續三個月維持月增0.3%，顯示整體通膨雖已脫
離高峰，服務類價格壓力仍具黏著性。能源與住房成本持續支撐通膨，反映需求與供給失衡
問題尚未完全改善，也使市場下修利率預期的空間受到限制。整體而言，本次數據傳遞「通
膨下降但速度偏慢」訊號，聯準會維持審慎立場的必要性仍高，市場將持續關注後續核心通
膨與勞動市場變化。
輝達
• 輝達共同創辦人黃仁勳於11月12日正式辭任執行長及董事職務，由財務長柯蕾特・克雷斯暫
代執行長，公司董事會則啟動接班人遴選。此次人事變動不僅涉及企業治理，更可能影響全
球AI產業的競爭格局。
• 輝達既有的研發團隊、CUDA開發者基礎及產品路線圖仍具延續性，短期營運不至於因人事更
迭立即出現結構性改變，後續關鍵在於接班團隊能否延續原有技術與生態系統優勢。

美債
• 2027年新國會就任後，兩黨將圍繞企業稅、富人稅以及部分即將到期的個人稅收減免展開拉
鋸。即便民主黨推動對企業與高收入者加稅，也極可能遭到白宮行使否決權，導致開源受阻
而節流難以達成。短期內，市場因預期大規模單邊財政刺激受阻，美債殖利率可能出現階段
性回落；但中長期看，6%以上的赤字率意味著美債供給壓力依然沉重，長端美債（10年期、
30年期）仍面臨較高的期限溢價。

顧
美國CPI月增放緩，核心物價僵固限制政策轉向空間
• 能源與住房價格推升整體通膨：10月CPI月增0.4%，雖較9月的0.6%放緩，但仍高於聯準會達成物價穩定所需水準，其
中能源價格月增0.7%、汽油月增1.0%，住房成本月增0.3%，兩者合計成為10月通膨主要推手，顯示地緣風險與住宅供
給不足仍持續壓抑通膨降溫速度。
• 核心通膨僵固反映服務需求仍具韌性：扣除食品與能源後的核心CPI月增0.3%，已連續三個月維持相同增幅；醫療服務、
汽車保險、教育及休閒服務價格持續上升，反映勞動市場與服務需求仍具支撐力；即使二手車價格月減1.2%，仍不足以
抵銷服務類價格壓力，顯示通膨回落進程可能較市場預期緩慢。
• 市場看法：本次數據傳達「通膨降溫但速度不足」訊號，市場可能提高聯準會維持高利率時間更長的預期，雖短期有利

美元與短天期美債殖利率維持高檔，但科技成長股評價將受利率壓力牽制。投組配置建議受惠高利率環境與能源價格支
撐的短天期美國公債、美元資產、高品質金融股及能源類股，並降低長天期債券曝險，以因應利率維持高檔風險。
CPI、核心CPI月增率趨勢
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.11。

輝達領導權更迭，短期營運不至於因人事更迭立即出現結構性改變
• 輝達領導權更迭，考驗AI生態系統的延續性：輝達共同創辦人黃仁勳於11月12日正式辭任執行長及董事職務，由財務長柯
蕾特・克雷斯暫代執行長，公司董事會則啟動接班人遴選。此次人事變動不僅涉及企業治理，更可能影響全球AI產業的競爭
格局。輝達的領先優勢並非僅來自GPU晶片性能，更建立在黃仁勳主導的整合策略與扁平化管理（約60名高階主管直接向黃
仁勳匯報），包括CUDA軟體平台、高速網路、AI伺服器及完整運算系統，形成難以複製的產業生態系。
• 短期營運不至於因人事更迭立即出現結構性改變：新任領導團隊無法維持跨部門整合效率，可能影響產品迭代速度與供應鏈
協調，為AMD等競爭對手創造追趕空間。不過，輝達既有的研發團隊、CUDA開發者基礎及產品路線圖仍具延續性，短期營

運不至於因人事更迭立即出現結構性改變，後續關鍵在於接班團隊能否延續原有技術與生態系統優勢。
事件時間軸與事件發生後主要主要機構評等與目標價
資料來源：各投行與評等機構，2026.11.13

輝達領導層變局影響：短期業績有撐，長期考驗技術決策速度
• 投資人最關心的公司盈利能力，輝達在未來一至兩年內仍具備極高的能見度，輝達的霸主地位並非僅建立在個人魅力之上，
而是龐大工程師團隊、難以撼動的CUDA軟體生態系統，以及緊密的全球半導體供應鏈。
• 短期財務預測不變：由於既有的客戶訂單、供應鏈產能分配與產品路線圖均已確立，預期這起人事變動不會改變輝達在
2027至2028年的核心獲利預測。
• 長期繼任風險浮現：黃仁勳過去能在晶片架構、軟體、網路與系統之間做出極快速的權衡決策，並深度維繫核心客戶與供應
商關係，失去這位靈魂人物後，繼任成本不會立刻反映在財報上，但可能會在未來數個產品週期中逐步顯現，考驗新管理層
在面對超微(AMD)等競爭對手時的應變與交易推進速度。
• 華為受限於製造瓶頸，短期難撼動輝達主導地位：在硬體算力供給方面，中國本土替代方案仍面臨嚴峻技術天花板。自

2020年美國切斷華為與台積電的代工合作後，華為被迫轉向中芯國際，不僅研發週期拉長，效能提升也相對有限。預估
2026年輝達的H100等效晶片產量高達2,300萬顆，而華為昇騰(Ascend)系列產量僅約90萬顆，不足輝達的 4%。
輝達主導全球AI晶片算力 華為2026年AI晶片產量預計不足輝達的4%
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.13

顧
美國對中國管制恐全面收緊
• 地緣政治與政策轉向：相較於公司內部營運交接，黃仁勳離任對美國出口管制政策的衝擊來得更為直接且劇烈。過去黃仁勳
是華府內部反對收緊對華晶片管制最具影響力的游說者，甚至能直接與總統川普溝通並促成政策放寬(如先前撤銷H20限制
與放行H200銷往中國)。隨著輝達在華盛頓的政治影響力減弱，美國政府與國會正加速封堵中國獲取先進AI算力的四大途徑
(本土生產、合法進口、轉運走私、海外遠程訪問)。即將於深圳舉行的APEC峰會前夕，美國強硬派立法動能增強，恐使明
年1月到期的中美貿易休戰協議更難延續，中國取得美國先進算力的管道可能在輝達內部繼任風險發酵前就已全面收緊。
• 小結與投資建議：黃仁勳的離任對全球AI產業而言是一場雙面刃衝擊，短期內，輝達憑藉深厚的技術護城河與訂單積壓，基

本面依然穩健；但中長期來看，公司將面臨領導層過渡的陣痛，同時因失去強力的華府游說管道，中國市場的營收貢獻與算
力供應鏈將面臨更嚴格的合規審查與政策封鎖，投資建議增持非中國區雲端服務商(CSP)與美國AI基礎設施標的，降低高度
依賴中國AI算力轉運或灰市貿易的周邊供應鏈。
中國獲取先進AI算力的四條途徑美國在前沿模型領域保持微弱領先
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.13

顧
中國出口續強，高科技需求推升貿易順差突破兆美元
• 中國出口續強，AI與高科技需求推升貿易順差突破兆美元：10月進出口數據優於預期，出口年增率35.2%較9月略為放緩，但
仍維持雙位數成長，進口年增41.6%，受惠於全球AI熱潮與科技投資擴張，積體電路出口金額年增率高達137.1%，不僅遠高
於整體出口增幅，也反映中國在半導體與電子供應鏈中的重要地位持續提升，此外近期美中雙方同意將部分關稅豁免措施再
延長兩個月，市場普遍解讀為雙邊貿易關係短期將保持和諧，此舉有助於降低企業接單與出貨的不確定性，維持電子、機械
設備、汽車零組件等與美國市場連結較深的產業的短期外銷動能。
• 展望後市：在AI需求帶動、高科技產品出口強勁，以及美中關稅延長帶來的政策利多支撐下，中國外貿仍展現韌性，不過，

若全球經濟成長放緩或地緣政治風險再度升溫，出口成長動能仍可能面臨挑戰，後續需持續觀察海外需求與貿易政策變化。
10月中國進出口數據優於預期

中國PPI加速上漲掩蓋了能源和科技領域外的通縮壓力
• 中國PPI加速上漲掩蓋了能源和科技領域外的通縮壓力：中國10月通膨數據顯示，經濟價格結構出現明顯分化，PPI年增率由
9月的5.0%升至5.7%，優於市場預期，且已連續第三個月走升，反映能源價格上漲及AI硬體成本增加推動上游價格回升，但
CPI年增率卻由0.9%降至0.7%，低於市場預估，從細項來看，10月價格回升主要集中於能源與科技硬體，然而食品價格跌幅
擴大，抵銷能源帶來的漲價效果，核心CPI由1.0%降至0.8%，反映一般消費需求與服務消費動能仍不足。
• 投資建議：市場預期中國GDP平減指數有機會自2022年以來首度轉正，但若缺乏更積極的財政刺激或消費支持政策，經濟多
數領域仍將面臨通縮壓力，後續需持續關注國際原物料價格走勢，及中國內需復甦力道對通膨的影響，後市來看AI供應鏈及

科技硬體族群仍具相對受惠機會。
資料來源:bloomberg,2026.11.10

國安基金激勵台股上週大漲，市場暫時看淡輝達換帥消息
• 國安基金第10次進場：11/6財政部宣布進場，為台股投資人注入信心，激勵台股上週週漲逾10%。國安基金表示，美國期
中選舉後主要股市重挫，加上外資延續10月以來賣超趨勢，短期內市場信心明顯不足，啟動成立以來第10次國安基金進場。
• 護盤持股聚焦少數核心權值股：國安基金早期操作傾向分散買入20檔左右，近幾次護盤則集中台積電為首的權值股。前次
護盤(第九次)台積電資金占比高達六成，其他權值股包含鴻海、日月光、聯發科、台達電、廣達、富邦金及台塑。
• 觀點：(1)從歷史績效統計來看，國安基金進場後短期大多能激勵台股上漲，但中期績效表現分歧且波動大，顯示總體經濟
趨勢才是關鍵。(2)AI運算晶片教父黃仁勳因婚姻醜聞宣布閃辭，市場初步認為需求基本面未變，台股不跌反漲。(3)整體來

說台股企業因此黃仁勳事件的衝擊不大，短線國安基金支撐股市信心，後市仍需聚焦在總體經濟環境及企業財報。在AI投資
反覆遭到市場質疑的環境下，建議持股聚焦具全球供應鏈龍頭地位的企業，適度減碼單純由消息面支撐的個股。
資料來源：財政部、Bloomberg，兆豐銀行整理。2026.11.13。

美債發行指引微調，期限結構調整彈性提升
• 刪除關鍵措辭，釋放長債供給調整訊號：美國財政部最新季度再融資聲明表示，未來數季預計維持名目公債與浮動利率
債券（FRN）標售規模不變，但將過去慣用的「至少維持目前規模」修改為「預計維持目前規模」，反映決策彈性提高。
市場解讀此舉代表財政部已開始為未來調整長天期公債供給預留政策空間，降低長債供給僵固性的訊號明顯。
• 兼顧融資成本與需求結構成為政策核心：財政部強調將持續評估不同期限債券的供需變化、聯準會縮表進程及發債成本，
並以「長期最低融資成本」為主要原則。在財政赤字仍大、短券需求強勁背景下，未來若長天期需求改善不足，不排除
透過調整期限結構降低利息負擔與市場吸收壓力。
• 後市觀察：若2027年後長債供給增速放緩，將有助緩解期限溢酬上升壓力，但強勁就業及通膨風險仍可能推升殖利率，

對20年以上美債及投資等級債券評價形成支撐。投資上可關注2至7年期美國公債及投資級債，兼顧票息與利率風險；
長債則留意美國20年以上公債、長存續期間投資級債，以等待供給政策與利率方向更明朗。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.11.10。Note: Expectations based on survey of primary
dealers designated by the Federal Reserve Bank of New York..

顧
民主黨期中選舉橫掃國會，但削減赤字的空間有限
• 民主黨期中選舉橫掃國會，但削減赤字的空間有限：從歷史經驗與2026年當前的財政結構來看，執政黨失去兩院主導權後，
美國財政赤字率（赤字占GDP比重）呈現短期擴張受限，但中長期難以下降的格局。截至2026財年（9月30日止），美國聯
邦赤字已達近 2兆美元（占GDP約 6.1%–6.2%），公眾持有債務突破 32兆美元（總債務破 40兆美元）。由於人口老化帶來
的社會安全（Social Security）與醫療保險（Medicare）屬於強制性支出，加上每年超過 1兆美元的債務利息負擔，兩黨在
不觸碰核心福利改革的情況下，削減赤字的空間有限。
• 美債供給壓力依然沉重：2027年新國會就任後，兩黨將圍繞企業稅、富人稅以及部分即將到期的個人稅收減免展開拉鋸。

即便民主黨推動對企業與高收入者加稅，也極可能遭到白宮行使否決權，導致開源受阻而節流難以達成。短期內，市場因預
期大規模單邊財政刺激受阻，美債殖利率可能出現階段性回落；但中長期看，6%以上的赤字率意味著美債供給壓力依然沉
重，長端美債（10年期、30年期）仍面臨較高的期限溢價。
資料來源：Silber bulletin，2026.11

顧 • AI資本支出短期壓抑現金流，亞洲供應鏈獲利動能持續擴散：美國超大型資料中心營運商未來幾年仍將持續擴大資本支出，
短期恐壓抑自由現金流，並引發市場對大型科技企業財務狀況的疑慮，導致美股科技股及亞洲科技、半導體類股短線波動。
然而，隨著全球AI資料中心建置持續推進，2027至2028年AI供應鏈需求預料仍將維持強勁，訂單與獲利動能亦持續向亞洲
擴散。摩根預估，亞洲科技企業自由現金流將由目前約2,450億美元增至2028年的6,330億美元；野村預估，台灣、日本及
韓國資訊科技指數2027年預估每股盈餘（EPS）成長率更分別達37%、41%及42%。
• 企業現金流改善挹注股東回饋，投資仍須留意評價與波動風險：充裕的自由現金流亦有助企業提升股東回饋，南韓與日本企
業本財政年度迄今回購金額分別達約140兆韓元及12.7兆日圓，進一步為股市提供支撐。儘管台灣、日本及韓國股市已累積

可觀漲幅，且美國聯準會（Fed）升息可能帶來評價壓力，但科技股中長期表現仍取決於企業獲利成長及預估上修動能。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國就業及企業活動持續改善，期中選舉牽動財政與科技監管政策 
經濟 
⚫ 10 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數由 54.5 升至 55.5，連續第 10 個
月維持於擴張區間；新訂單、生產及庫存表現改善，顯示需求與生產動
能仍具韌性。惟投入成本仍處高檔，且關稅政策、地緣政治及供應鏈風
險猶存，後續發展仍有待進一步觀察。 
⚫ 10 月美國 ISM 服務業採購經理人指數為 54.5，較上月 54.9 略降 0.4 個
百分點，但已連續 28 個月維持擴張，服務業景氣持續增長。新訂單與
商業活動雖趨緩，就業續呈擴張，未完成訂單創 2022 年 7 月以來高點；
新出口訂單重返擴張區間，顯示內外需求仍具韌性，惟關稅、能源價格
及成本壓力仍為後續觀察重點。 
⚫ 10 月美國非農就業增加 16.5 萬人，高於上月修正後之 6.0 萬人，失業
率由 4.2%降至 4.1%，加以前兩個月就業人數合計上修 8.4 萬人，顯示
勞動市場較前期有所回溫。惟新增就業仍主要集中於專業及商業服務、
醫療保健及運輸倉儲等部分服務業，製造業增幅相對有限，反映勞動市
場雖維持韌性，但部分就業品質及企業用工需求仍存在壓力。 
⚫ 9 月美國職位空缺數由 719.0 萬個增至 752.3 萬個，職缺率由 4.3%升至
4.5%，企業徵才需求有所回升；聘僱人數亦同步增加，聘僱率由 3.3%
升至 3.4%。整體而言，企業用人需求改善、裁員規模下降，勞動市場仍
維持穩健，供需情勢持續朝平衡方向發展。 
⚫ 9 月美國商品及服務貿易逆差 1,050 億美元，較上月減少 0.7%。出口成
長 1.9%，高於進口增幅 1.2%，主因資本財及黃豆等農產品出口增加；
進口則受資本財及消費財需求帶動。累計 1 至 9 月貿易逆差較去年同期
縮減 12.2%，顯示對外貿易失衡情況有所改善。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 2026 年美國期中選舉呈現反執政黨效應，民主黨重掌國會主導權。截至
11 月 8 日開票結果，民主黨已確定取得參、眾兩院多數席次，其中參議
院獲 54 席、共和黨 44 席；眾議院則由民主黨取得 254 席、共和黨 162
席。州長席次方面，民主黨掌握 28 席，共和黨 20 席，惟部分選區仍在
計票，最終席次尚待確認。民主黨在多個關鍵州選戰中獲勝，反映選民
對高物價、能源價格及中東局勢等議題的不滿情緒升高。選後國會由民

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.11.9 
一、 美國—就業及企業活動持續改善，期中選舉牽動財政與科技監管政策 
2026 年 10 月美國非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，明顯高於上月修正後之 6.0
萬人，亦高於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.8 萬人之水準，企業聘僱動能有所回
升；失業率則由 4.2%降至 4.1%，持續維持於 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%之區間，勞動
市場整體仍具穩定性。產業別方面，新增就業主要由專業及商業服務業增加 7.0
萬人、醫療保健業增加 4.2 萬人及運輸倉儲業增加 2.5 萬人所帶動，製造業亦增
加 1.9 萬人，其餘產業就業變動幅度相對有限。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升
至 38.0 美元，月增 0.4%、年增 3.1%，增幅較前月略有加快；平均每週工時則維
持 34.4 小時不變。此外，8 月及 9 月新增就業人數合計上修 8.4 萬人，反映上兩
月勞動市場表現較初估時有所改善。整體而言，10 月美國新增就業明顯回升、失
業率小幅下降，加上前兩個月就業數據獲得上修，勞動市場有所改善，整體仍具
韌性；惟新增就業主要集中於部分服務業，且因經濟因素從事兼職工作的人數增
加，反映勞動市場仍存在就業不足及產業表現不均的情況。 
2026 年 9 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings)由上月修正後之 719.0 萬個增加
33.3 萬個至 752.3 萬個，職缺率由 4.3%升至 4.5%，企業徵才需求略有回溫；聘僱
人數由 527.1 萬人增加至 540.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.3%升至 3.4%。總離職人數由
517.5 萬人增加至 531.9 萬人，離職率維持於 3.3%。就離職結構觀察，主動離職
(Quits)人數由 304.4 萬人增加至 336.5 萬人，主動離職率由 1.9%升至 2.1%，勞工
轉職意願有所回升；裁員與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges)人數則由 176.8 萬人減少
至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由 1.1%降至 1.0%，企業人力調整壓力仍屬溫和。產業別方
面，職位空缺增加主要來自州及地方政府(不含教育)、專業與商業服務業、製造
業及營建業；聘僱人數則以住宿與餐飲服務業增幅較為明顯；醫療保健與社會救
助業裁員人數則有所下降。整體而言，9 月美國職缺數與聘僱人數同步增加，主
動離職意願回升且裁員規模下降，顯示企業用人需求有所改善，勞動市場仍維持
穩健，惟供需關係持續朝較為平衡方向發展。 
2026 年 10 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI)由上月的
54.5 升至 55.5，增加 1.0 點，連續第 10 個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，製造業景

氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由 55.3 升至 55.5，接單情況持續改善；生產
指數由 56.7 攀升至 59.0，製造活動進一步增溫；就業指數雖由 52.7 微降至 52.4，
但仍維持擴張。供應商交貨指數由 59.0 微升至 59.1，反映交貨時間持續延長；庫
存指數由 48.6 升至 51.4，顯示企業備貨意願回升。此外，價格指數由 77.9 降至
75.2，投入成本壓力雖略有緩和但仍處高檔；未完成訂單指數雖由 56.4 降至 54.6，
但仍在擴張區間。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由 50.9 升至 51.2，進口指數由 51.0
升至 52.9，均維持擴張區間，顯示外需及企業採購需求仍具支撐。整體而言，10
月美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、生產及庫存表現改善，顯示需求與生
產動能仍具韌性；惟投入成本仍處相對高檔，加上關稅政策、地緣政治情勢及供
應鏈變數猶存，後續對企業成本控制及製造業景氣發展之影響仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 10 月美國 ISM 服務業採購經理人指數(Services PMI)為 54.5，較上月
54.9 下降 0.4 個百分點，惟已連續第 28 個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，且高於近
12 個月平均值 54.3，美國服務業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，商業活動指數由 56.5
降至 53.8，新訂單指數由 59.8 降至 55.9，顯示需求與營運動能較前月放緩，但仍
維持擴張；就業指數由 50.1 升至 51.4，連續第 2 個月處於擴張區間，反映企業用
人需求仍具韌性。供應商交貨指數由 53.2 升至 56.7，交貨時間進一步延長；價格
指數由 74.0 降至 73.0，雖較前月回落，但已連續 23 個月高於 60，企業成本壓力
仍高。庫存指數由 57.8 降至 55.4，未完成訂單指數則由 56.6 升至 58.0，創 2022
年 7 月以來最高，顯示需求累積情形持續增加。此外，新出口訂單指數由 46.9 大
幅升至 55.2，重返擴張區間，進口指數亦由 52.9 升至 54.2，顯示外部需求及採購
活動回溫。ISM 指出，關稅及燃料成本仍是企業最關注的供應鏈議題，許多企業
尚未完全將增加成本轉嫁給客戶，未來仍須持續關注關稅、能源價格及成本壓力
對服務業景氣與通膨之影響；惟值得注意的是，10 月所有主要指標均位於擴張區
間，為 2026 年 2 月以來首見，顯示整體服務業景氣基礎仍具支撐。 
2026 年 9 月美國商品及服務貿易逆差為 1,050 億美元，較上月減少 7 億美元
(-0.7%)。當月出口 3,215 億美元，較上月增加 58 億美元(+1.9%)；進口 4,266 億美
元，增加 52 億美元(+1.2%)，出口增幅高於進口，使貿易逆差略為改善。其中，
商品貿易逆差縮減至 1,359 億美元(-0.5%)，服務貿易順差略降至 309 億美元(-
0.1%)。出口成長主要受資本財出口增加 38 億美元帶動，其中電腦、電腦周邊設
備及民用航空器表現較佳；食品、飼料及飲料出口亦增加 19 億美元，以黃豆增幅

6

最為顯著。進口方面，資本財進口增加 116 億美元，消費財進口增加 24 億美元，
惟工業用品及原材料進口減少 37 億美元。累計 2026 年 1 至 9 月，美國商品及服
務貿易逆差為 6,623 億美元，較 2025 年同期減少-12.2%；同期出口成長 11.6%，
進口增加 6.2%，顯示對外貿易失衡情況較去年同期有所改善。 
2026 年美國期中選舉呈現反執政黨效應，民主黨重掌國會主導權，截至 2026
年 11 月 8 日，美國期中選舉開票結果顯示，民主黨已確定取得國會參、眾兩院
控制權。其中，參議院民主黨陣營取得 54 席，共和黨 44 席；眾議院民主黨取得
254 席，共和黨 162 席。此外，全美州長席次分布方面，民主黨掌握 28 席，共和
黨 20 席。由於部分選區仍在計票或尚未確認選舉結果，最終席次仍待確定。民主
黨並於全國眾議院普選票(Popular Vote)獲得較高得票率，且在德州、堪薩斯州、
北卡羅來納州、俄亥俄州及南卡羅來納州等關鍵州選戰取得突破，反映選民對高
物價、能源價格上漲、中東衝突及部分政策方向的不滿情緒升高。此次選舉終結
共和黨自 2024 年總統大選以來同時掌控白宮與國會兩院的局面，美國再度形成
「分裂政府」(Divided Government)格局。未來川普政府推動重要法案、預算案、
人事任命及稅制改革等政策，將面臨更大的國會制衡與協商壓力，民主黨亦可能
加強對行政部門的監督與調查。就經濟層面而言，市場預期大規模減稅、財政擴
張及部分貿易政策推動速度可能放緩，有助於降低財政赤字進一步擴大的風險；
惟兩黨對立加深，亦可能提高預算協商、債務上限及政府融資等議題的不確定性。 
美國民主黨於 2026 年 11 月期中選舉取得國會參、眾兩院控制權後，未來人
工智慧監管政策走向受到關注。其中，由無黨籍參議員桑德斯（Bernie Sanders）
及民主黨眾議員卡薩爾（Greg Casar）於 9 月分別提出的《2026 年禁止人工超級
智慧法案》（Ban Artificial Superintelligence Act of 2026），主張禁止開發、部署、
持有及資助具備超越人類認知能力或可能威脅人類安全的人工超級智慧（ASI），
並設立內閣層級的聯邦「人工智慧部」（Department of Artificial Intelligence），
統籌先進 AI 系統的安全審查及監管。此外，法案規劃在監管機構完成籌設並建
立相關安全規範前，暫停訓練運算量達 10²⁵次以上的先進 AI 系統進行訓練、修改
及微調，並限制未發布系統的部署。截至 11 月 8 日，該法案仍處於國會委員會
審議階段，尚未正式立法。隨著民主黨將於 2027 年 1 月取得國會主導權，後續
法案審議進展及其對美國 AI 技術發展、企業投資與國際競爭的影響，值得持續
關注。

二、歐元區—通膨壓力持續增強 
 歐元區 10 月 HICP 通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 4%，核心通膨(不含食品及能源)
則持平為 2.5%。該月食品及能源通膨皆持續上揚，反映國際原油價格居高不下，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品價格。另外，服務通膨上升 0.1 個百
分點至 3.3%，部分反映交通費用上漲的影響，惟服務通膨仍是低於今年上半年的
平均水準。展望未來，近期布蘭特原油雖回落至 100 美元內，但美伊談判陷入僵
局，仍預期年底前布蘭特原油位於每桶 90 美元以上，將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後
續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，
進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含：(1)歐元區 9 月失業率上升 0.1 個百分點為 6.5%，失業人
數增加 12.2 萬人，主要是法國失業人數增加較多所致，係因大規模抗議活動的影
響；(2)歐元區 9 月零售額月減 0.7%、第三季季減 0.5%，物價上漲帶來的負面影
響逐步顯現，預期第四季零售額將延續此一疲態。 
三、日本—薪資成長動能增強，春鬥效應持續擴散 
 9 月薪資年增 4.6%，創 1997 年以來最大增幅，除受夏季獎金大增 32.6%支撐
外，經常性薪資亦年增 3.7%，顯示今年春鬥的加薪成果正逐步由大型企業擴散至
中小企業。隨企業獲利保持強勁，加上日本最大工會聯合組織（Rengo）已表態明
年將爭取更高加薪幅度，預期薪資成長動能仍可延續，有助支撐民間消費，但同
時也可能推升服務價格，增加通膨持續性。 
四、中國—關注 11 中旬川習會發展 
10 月官方製造業 PMI 下降 0.8 點至 49.3，主因十一長假前需求提前釋放，以
及外部環境不確定性升高所致。新訂單、生產指數分別降至 49.4、49.8，新出口
訂單亦降至 48.7。此外，生產及出廠價格指數分別降至 56.8 及 51.7，反映成本與
售價漲幅已有收斂跡象；惟就業指數則持平於 48.4，顯示就業仍顯疲弱。不過，
RatingDog 製造業 PMI 略增 0.1 點至 52.1，反映 RatingDog 調查以中小型出口企
業為主，在全球 AI 相關需求的支持下，仍能保持穩定的擴張表現；惟 RatingDog
製造業產出物價指數連續兩個月下調，投入物價指數則持續上揚，反映競爭環境
激烈，迫使廠商犧牲利潤以降價求售。

官方非製造業 PMI 下降 0.5 點至 49.7，其中建築業商務活動指數由 50.3 降至
48.0，服務業商務活動指數則小幅下降至 50，顯示十一長假消費動能並不特別活
躍；非製造業就業指數雖微升至 46.0，但仍遠低於榮枯線，不利於後續內需復甦。
另外，RatingDog 服務業指數下降 0.8 點至 50.8，亦反映需求增速減弱，使得
RatingDog 綜合產出指數為 52，較 9 月的 52.4 回落，為近三個月最慢，製造業產
出增快為能抵銷服務業擴張放緩。 
展望未來，中國製造業仍面臨外需不確定、內需復甦偏弱及價格競爭激烈等，
惟全球 AI 產業需求可望擴張，加上人行上調外匯存款準備率，有助緩和人民幣
升值對出口競爭力的壓力，接有助於繼續支持出口需求。此外，若 11 月 APEC 期
間川習會釋出改善中美經貿關係訊號，將有助提振企業信心及出口前景，惟後續
仍須觀察全球需求及地緣政治發展。 
五、印度及東南亞—馬來西亞央行按兵不動；印尼、越南出口亮眼；泰國通膨壓
力上升 
(1) 印尼：9 月出口額達 275.9 億美元，創四年新高、年增 11.87%，非油氣出口較
去年成長 12.39%，以動植物油脂成長 89.04%為最，礦物燃料及電氣機械及設
備分別成長 12%、24.37%，抵銷石油和天然氣出口下滑 1%的影響，對美國、
中國出口分別成長 23.21%、11.82%。9 月進口額達 242.3 億美元、年增 18.41%，
增幅創 6 個月新低，其中成品油進口成長 37%，非石油和天然氣進口額年增
17.59%，以電氣機械設備(25.9%)及塑膠製品(16.3%)增幅較大，自中國進口額
大增 30.23%、自東協和歐盟進口分別成長 20.98%、18.21%。總計 9 月，貿易
順差為 33.6 億美元，2026 年前 9 個月，貿易順差 106.1 億美元，出口和進口
分別成長 5.58%和 19.67%。 
(2) 泰國：10 月消費者物價年增率(CPI)從 9 月的 2.82%飆升至 3.9%，3 年多來首
次突破泰國央行 1%-3%的目標區間，主要受油價上漲和洪水導致食品價格上
漲的影響，泰國商務部預計 2026 年整體通膨率平均在 1.8%-2.2%之間，但由
於基期較低，物價壓力可能在年底前持續走高。雖然通膨壓力顯著增強，但官
方評估此波物價飆漲應屬暫時現象。 
(3) 馬來西亞：馬來西亞央行(BNM)11 月會議將隔夜政策利率（OPR）維持在 2.75%
不變，為連續第八次維持利率不變，符合市場預期。政策聲明強化對經濟成長

的樂觀評估，表示在強勁的出口表現和持續的內需支撐下，2026 年全年經濟

(4) 越南: 10 月出口額年增 51.4%，達 646.7 億美元的歷史新高，其中電子產品出
口年增 102.6%為主要成長動力，機械設備出口年增 49.8%居次。同時，10 月
進口額達 573.6 億美元、年增幅略放緩至 43.1%，其中，電子產品進口成長
84.5%，能源進口則因油價上揚而大幅增加 74.1%。總計 10 月，貿易順差為
73.1 億美元，連續第二個月轉為順差；2026 年前 10 月，貿易逆差小幅收斂至
120.7 億美元，出口額成長放緩至 27.5%、進口額成長率為 37.4%。10 月 CPI
年通膨率小幅上升至 5.09%，創 5 月以來最高，其中，以交通運輸費用上漲
15.57%為最，酒精飲料和菸草（4.40%）、住房和公用事業（6.50%）及醫療保
健（1.75%）價格漲幅均有所加快，由於通膨已連三月突破央行目標區間，將
加大升息的壓力。 
六、台灣—通膨漲幅收斂，尚無輸入性通膨疑慮 
10 月消費者物價指數（CPI）年增 2.33%，較 9 月的 2.74%明顯回落，顯示整
體通膨壓力已有降溫跡象，但仍近半年皆高於 2%。10 月物價漲幅收斂，主要受
蔬菜價格轉跌、電價漲幅縮小，以及部分商品與服務價格漲勢趨緩等因素影響，
惟外食費年增 3.01%，已連續 5 個月超過 3%，民眾對物價上漲的感受仍較明顯。
另，10 月以新台幣計價的進口物價年增 33.32%，創近 50 年最大漲幅，主要受國
際原油及 AI 需求推升電子零組件價格所致，惟相關電子零組件多供出口使用，
對國內消費端影響有限，加以政府採取油價平穩措施，因此主計總處認為目前並
無輸入性通膨問題。展望未來，隨中秋節比較基期因素消退，主計總處預估 11 月
CPI 年增率仍將高於 2%，且漲幅可能較 10 月擴大。

研 究 報 告                
義隆(2458.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
深受 NB 出貨趨緩影響，短期營運受壓抑 
 投資評價與建議 
NB客⼾拉貨⼒道趨弱將壓抑整體營運動能，無⼈機相關產品為主要成⻑來源，
2027年營運展望保守，給予30倍PER*2027年EPS評價。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26 NB產業承壓，營運同步面臨逆風：NB客⼾拉貨保守，雖有短/急單，但
延續性恐不佳，僅有無⼈機相關產品表現較佳，推估營收16億元，QoQ-27.9%，
且仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓⼒，推估毛利率43.9%，EPS 0.51元。 
短期NB缺料問題難解，2027年營運成⻑動能恐受限：展望2027年，IDC預估
PC出貨量YoY-4.1%，而義隆營收約80%以上與NB相關，因此短期公司營運亦
同處於逆風，主要動能來自非NB產品的無⼈機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身
演算法優勢，推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP產品，1H26出貨
以ISP為主，ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，
已通過雷虎測試並開始出貨，2H26 ASP明顯提升，為2026年最佳成⻑動能，
也是推升AI應用占比提升主要動能，2026年AI營收YoY+40%，也是未來公司
重心，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛⼒。整體而
言，短期NB客⼾拉貨恐保守，主要仰賴無⼈機等非NB產品推升，營運成⻑較
受限。 
 投資風險 
NB需求進⼀步下滑或非NB貢獻不如預期。

| 營運概況 
3Q26營運受NB影響而趨緩，獲利低於預期：3Q26營收22.2億元，QoQ-30.8%，主因1H26 NB客⼾提前拉貨及
缺料造成拉貨⼒道下滑，毛利率44.7%，主因產品組合轉差及供應鏈成本提升(包含模組的被動元件及PCB漲價)，
費用因嚴控成本而下滑至6.96億元，業外主要來自金融評價損失，EPS 0.58元，優於預期。 
4Q26 NB產業承壓，營運同步面臨逆風：NB客⼾面臨CPU、記憶體缺料問題，拉貨保守，雖有短/急單，但延續
性恐不佳，僅有無⼈機相關產品表現較佳，推估營收16億元，QoQ-27.9%，且仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓⼒，推估
毛利率43.9%，EPS 0.51元。 
短期NB缺料問題難解，2027年營運成⻑動能恐受限：受記憶體與CPU缺貨持續壓抑NB出貨成⻑性，以及NB漲價、
AI排擠商用NB需求等因素，雖1H26提前拉貨推升公司營運仍佳，然自3Q26起NB客⼾需求趨緩，使得公司營運逐
季下滑，展望2027年，IDC預估PC出貨量YoY-4.1%，而義隆營收約80%以上與NB相關，因此短期公司營運亦同
處於逆風，主要動能來自1)Haptic滲透率持續提升，因Haptic ASP達15美元，明顯優於傳統clickpad 2美元，將
帶動觸控板ASP提升，2026年Haptic營收貢獻約3.5~4億元(2025年約0.6億元)，2027年也將持續提升；2)ADAS
來自電動巴士，2026年出貨double，已有第六位客⼾開始小量出貨，2027年持續成⻑，主要來自政府電動巴士
補助所推升，政府最近也補助電動物流車，2027年確實落地後，公司將有電動物流車的成⻑機會，量也比電動巴
士多很多；3)無⼈機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身演算法優勢，推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP
產品，1H26出貨以ISP為主，ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，已通過雷虎測試並
開始出貨，目前僅作前端光學模組，2026年底將推出避障功能，四鏡頭方案約500美元，十二鏡頭方案將達1,000
美元，2H26 ASP明顯提升，為2026年最佳成⻑動能，也是推升AI應用占比提升主要動能，2026年AI營收YoY+40%，
也是未來公司重心，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛⼒。整體而言，短期NB客⼾拉貨
恐保守，主要仰賴無⼈機等非NB產品推升，營運成⻑較受限。 
2027年營運動能受限，維持中立評等：NB客⼾拉貨⼒道趨弱將壓抑整體營運動能，無⼈機相關產品為主要成⻑來
源，2027年營運展望保守，維持中立評等，給予30倍PER*2027年EPS評價。

### Page 6

Inventec (2356.TW)
10 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Inventec
Company description
Established in 1975 and listed in 1996, Taiwan-based Inventec is a key ODM
of NBs, servers, smartphones, and wearable/IoT devices to global customers.
Major shareholders include the founder Mr. Yeh Kuo-I, whose family has (both
directly and indirectly) a c35% stake.
Investment strategy
We rate Inventec shares as Buy. We expect stable demand in NB and server to
help support near-term earnings, while product diversification within smart

devices to areas such as IoT and medical space should help improve L-T
margins. We expect the company's solid cash yield to serve as a downside
cushion.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$65 for Inventec shares is based on 17x 2027 EPS, which
is slightly above the mid-point of its historical trading range, reflecting the
company's product diversification and stable demand outlook led by servers
and commercial NBs, as well as increasing AI server exposure that could

potentially drive margin expansion and better earnings momentum.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) Slower-than-expected shipment growth/weaker-than-expected
sell-through in HomePods and AirPods could lead to downside in our sales
forecasts; 2) If competitors adopt aggressive pricing strategies to gain market
shares, our ASP and margin assumptions could face downside risk; 3) Lower-
than-expected server margins driven by rising exposure to Chinese customers

could impact our earnings forecasts negatively; 4) Deterioration in NB
demand and investment in solar would hurt our forecasts; and 5) Stronger-
than-expected Taiwan dollar could lead to FX loss and downside to our
current forecasts.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Gold Circuit

Reiterate Buy: 3Q26 margin beat; ongoing 
sales/margin growth; trim PO to NT$1,250

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,250 TWD | Price: 983.00 TWD

3Q26 beat: solid margins despite lighter sales 
Gold Circuit (GCE)’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 38.7%/30.6% exceeded BofAe of 35.9%/27.8% 
and consensus of 36.9%/28.9%, despite lighter sales (+22% QoQ, +67% YoY; 5%/3% 
below BofAe/consensus). OP (+48% QoQ, +95% YoY) was 4%/3% above 
BofAe/consensus. We attribute the strong core results to a still favorable mix and lower 
material cost burden. In 3Q26, AI made up 50%+ of sales, vs. 30-40%/40-50% in 
1Q/2Q26. Server/networking comprised 77%/17% of 3Q26 sales, vs. 72-78%/15-20% in 
1Q-2Q26. With a non-operating loss (details to be disclosed; likely FX loss in our view) 
amid a lighter tax rate, NP was 9%/7% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3).  
Sales momentum intact; margin expansion continues 
We now expect 4Q26/1Q27 sales to rise 2%/3% QoQ & 2027/28 sales to rise 41%/31% 
YoY, driven by ASP growth (for spec upgrade and higher material cost) and ongoing 
capacity expansion in Taiwan/Thailand/China. GCE’s Oct sales (+6% MoM, +76% YoY) 
reinforce our confidence in its order and project pipeline. We also note that the impacts 
from AWS’ project pipeline change and shipping disruptions due to the flood in Thailand 
are fading. We now assume GM to expand from 36.8% in 2026E to 38.0%/38.3% in 
2027/28E, after considering higher material cost pass-through, higher depreciation costs 
from new facilities, and higher upfront costs for making next-generation HDI products.  
Cut PO to NT$1,250 with a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We trim 2026-27E EPS by 1-4% but lift 2028E EPS by 4%, mainly to reflect the 3Q26 
results beat, stronger margin profile, but higher share count. We lower our PO to 
NT$1,250 (21x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,500 (25x 2027E EPS), with the valuation base 
unchanged and a lower P/E multiple. We apply 21x (c.1.5SD above P/E average since 
2020), vs. prior 25x (2.5SD above) on valuation de-rating across AI supply chain amid 
macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on GCE as a top ASIC server PCB 
maker, with all-time focus on high-end AI server/networking and expanding margins.

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
Gold Circuit (GCE) is a PCB company in Taiwan with high 
exposure in server (74% of 2025 sales) and networking 
(15%). Key end-clients are global CSPs and US top 
networking/telecom names. Besides its headquarters, the 
company has production sites in Taoyuan (Taiwan), Suzhou 
(China), Jiangsu (China), and Thailand, with ongoing capacity 
expansion. GCE was established in 1981 and listed on the 
Taiwan stock market in March 1998.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Gold Circuit, given (1) its high 
exposure and focus on better-margin server/networking 
business amid rising AI applications globally to keep high 
ASP/margin, (2) strong competitive advantages and high 
entry barrier as focusing on high-end products, (3) higher 
production flexibility after adding capacity in Thailand, (4) 
positive FCF/OCF since 2007/2019, respectively, with an 
improving balance sheet (from net debt to net cash) and 
expanding ROE.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Gold Circuit (GOCCF) 
Our PO of NT$1,250 is based on 21x 2027E EPS. The 21x valuation multiple is around 
1.5SD above the average level since 2020 when better-margin server business started to 
account for 50%+ of total sales, leading to an improved 20%+ GM profile. We believe 
the P/E multiple target can be justified by (1) strong AI PCB market demand, (2) GCE's 
high AI server exposure, (3) solid sales mix from better-margin server/networking 
business, and (4) ongoing margin expansion to historical high level.

Upside risks: (1) stronger server/networking market demand to drive ASP/margins, (2) 
better utilization rate, (3) faster capacity expansion in Thailand, (4) lower raw 
material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) favorable FX movement.

Downside risks: (1) ASIC platform delay, (2) weaker market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) softer AI server demand due to AI chip short supply, (4) GM pressure 
from lower utilization rate, higher raw material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) unfavorable 
FX movement.

AllRing Tech Co.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26   margin miss from product
mix
3Q26 earnings missed our estimate by 13% and consensus by 16%, given less
favorable product mix...   There was less  inspection (gross margin >60%) but more
automation tools (gross margin ~40%).
...as well as weaker subsidiary company performance.
Looking into 4Q26, we expect gross margin to recover to >50% from improving
product mix: However, this will still be slightly offset by new product shipped to

the OSATs, which carries lower margin in the early ramping phase.
We think CoWoS should still account for 70-80% of 2027e total revenue – with
other new customers and tools, including a US customer, CPO, and CoW process.
We think CPO could account for 15-20% of 2027e revenue, with shipments up >10x
from 2026.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AllRing Tech Co. (6187.TWO)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions are:
n Cost of equity (CoE) of 8%, derived from a risk-free rate of 2.0%, beta of 1.0, and a
market risk premium of 6.0%.
n Intermediate growth rate of 16%.
n Cash dividend payout of 65%
n Terminal growth rate of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected CoWoS capacity expansion
n Faster-than-expected adoption of silicon photonics

n Market share gains from peers, led by continuous R&D and China's localization trend
Risks to Downside
n Slowdown in CoWoS capacity expansion
n Allring's "WoS" market share declining
n Slowdown in technology migration such as SoIC and silicon photonics adoption

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

ab 12 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
Accton Technology Corporation
Q4 GM to see strong recovery
Q3 GM  down 2.1pp QoQ on higher component cost
Accton reported Q326 EPS of NT$23.39, below UBS/Consensus estimates on softer
margins. Q326 sales of NT$120.7bn (up 26% QoQ) was already posted, and slightly
below UBSe as its  AI card business (Trainium 3) shifted to consignment model for certain
higher cost components from mid-August. Q326 GM of 17.6% was down 2.1ppts QoQ

due to   higher component cost and ~1.5 months of  consignment, and we believe this
should be temporary as its customer will reimburse them in the subsequent quarters. Q3
OPEX increased by  15% QoQ, primarily due to higher R&D expenses, resulted in OPM of
13.4% (-1.6ppts QoQ). We estimate data center switch accounted for ~37% of Q326
sales (vs 36% in  Q226), while accelerator card was ~50% of total sales (50% in Q226).
Q4  growth on track despite full quarter of consignment

Accton expects Q426F revenue to grow high-single digit % QoQ with potential upside
for low-teens % QoQ growth, subject to component supply (i.e. switch IC) and
customers' year-end inventory adjustment, despite a full quarter of component
consignment (vs 1.5 month in Q3). It sees robust demand from  cloud switch  with the
social media customer revenue contribution exceeded e-commerce customer from
Q326, which is likely to sustain into Q426F. It also noted the social media customer has

secured more switch chipsets from Marvell and Nvidia Spectrum, and continues to
negotiate with Broadcom for better allocation. Accton also sees better unit and content
growth for Trainium 3 as the customer ramps liquid-cooled design, which carry ~20%
higher ASP and better GM due to increased design complexity. We forecast Q426E sales
growth of 9% QoQ, while its GM should recover to 19.9% (+2.3 ppt QoQ) on more
favourable mix and its customer reimbursement on the Q3 prepaid component costs.

Aggressive capacity expansion to support mid-term  growth
Accton announced  US$226 mn (~NT$7.3 bn) new capex on 12 Nov. for capacity
expansion in Taiwan and Vietnam, as well as construction of new fab/cleanroom in
Malaysia and Singapore. It aims to fund the capex through internal cashflow, bank
loans,  and has announced a 10mn shares of rights issuance (~2% dilution) on 22 Oct.
We believe this will drive its overall capacity to grow 40-50% YoY in 2027E, supporting

overall 2027E revenue to grow ~35% YoY, driven by 1) further ramp of Trainium 3 with
higher adoption of liquid cooling, 2) sustained 800G switch demand and migration to
1.6T with new customer addition, 3) increasing contribution from Optical Switches.
Valuation: Reiterate Buy with NT$3,200 PT
We  fine tune our model and raise   2026-28E EPS by 0-2%. We retain  Buy but lower  PT to
NT$3,200 (from   NT$3,688) based on 22x  average 2027-28E PE (from 26x) on overall

market valuation compression. We believe recent concerns over switch IC  shortages and
GM pressure from higher component costs are overdone as we expect Accton's GM to
recover from Q426E. Accton will host its annual investor conference in late November.

Company Description
Founded in 1988, Accton specialises in ODM/OEM solutions for data center switches, AI
accelerator cards, enterprise/ODM switches, carrier access/telecom and others. Accton is one
of the largest vendors of white-box and OCP-compliant switches globally (by sales).

Leveraging deep expertise in high-speed switching, system integration and rapid time-to-
market, it has secured long-term partnerships with major cloud service providers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Accton using PE methodology.
Key risks include: 1) weaker-than-expected demand for data center switches; 2) slower ramp
of 800G and 1.6T data center switches; 3) market share loss in the AI accelerator card
business; and 4) softer gross margin than expected.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Document 196: 261113_daiwa_ Lotes

Source PDF: all report pdf/261113_daiwa_ Lotes.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We review Lotes’s 3Q26 results, released on 13 November.

What’s the impact: Business outlook. Ongoing gross margin dilution due 
to rising raw material costs and less benefits from the AI server trend has 
been, in our view, the primary drag on Lotes’s share price over this year. 
We believe this trend may reverse now as investors are worried about the 
potential AI capex cuts impacting the overall Taiwan supply chain’s revenue 
outlook in 2028, whereas Lotes only has 3-5% of exposure currently. Also, 
management expects gross margin pressure to start easing with the new 
product mass production (eg, SOCAMM) starting from 4Q26. Second, 
Lotes’s regular server-related products may see less chance for inventory 
correction as its products are not in shortage now. Lastly, its desktop 
business has suffered more than the overall industry’s headwinds (ie, unit 
decline 10-15% YoY) as its desktop products had to downgrade from DDR5 
to DDR4/3 due to component shortages, which further negatively impacted 
profitability. We believe these three headwinds will gradually transform into 
tailwinds once the AI capex starts to slow down. However, if the AI server 
capex trend remains as is, we could also see some upside for the 
company. Lotes is still eager to develop a GPU socket for the Rubin Ultra 
generation, which we believe could become a new growth driver. Besides, 
quick disconnect (QD) and NPO sockets might also become growth drivers. 
While QD only accounts for low single digit of total revenue from 3Q26, we 
expect it to have a chance to increase to c.10% of total revenue in 2027E.

3Q26 profitability missed expectations. Lotes’s 3Q26 earnings came in 
at TWD1.92bn (EPS: TWD17.1), which was 11%/14% lower than our/street 
estimates. Despite revenue largely in-line with expectations, gross margin 
decreased by 0.6pp QoQ to 44.8%, mainly from rising copper and gold 
prices; unfavourable product mix (rising high-speed cable and connect and 
QD) may be another reason. However, operating margin rose 0.9pp QoQ to 
22.9% mainly on the lack of one-off employee stock bonus expense (as 
done in 2Q26) but opex was still higher than the company’s guidance 
(<TWD2.0bn) due to salary increase for its Chinese branches.

What we recommend: We lower 2026-27E EPS by 2-7% to factor in 3Q26 
results and lower profitability recovery assumption but maintain 2028E 
EPS. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call but lower our 12M TP to TWD2,480 (from 
TWD2,850), based on a lower PER of 22x (from 28x; high end of its past-5-
year range of 10-28x), on our 1-year forward EPS, given the weaker market 
sentiment. Key risk: lower-than-expected adoption of server CPU platforms.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E revenues are 5-10% higher than the 
Bloomberg consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on QDs.

13 November 2026

3Q26 review: bottomed-out gross margin

➢ 3Q GM slightly declined on rising material cost but likely bottomed out 
➢ Rising QD, SOCAMM, NPO sockets to be key growth drivers in 2027 
➢ Reiterating our Buy (1) rating; lowering 12-month TP to TWD2,480

Company profile 
Founded in 1986 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2007, Lotes is one of the world’s leading 
connector vendors specialising in CPU socket/ILM and connectors, such as DDR, PCIe, SATA, and 
Type-C, with most of its production capacities at its Guangzhou, Zhongshan and Suzhou plants. Its 
superior manufacturing capability and strong client relationships are the key differentiators that make 
Lotes stand out from its competitors. Since the PC market became saturated from 2015, Lotes has 
focused on penetrating non-PC connector businesses, such as connectors for servers, automotive and 
industrial, and new types of cables (eg, Thunderbolt cable).

### Page 5

5

Lotes (3533 TT): 13 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 13 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Lotes’ board consists of 7 directors, including 3 independent directors. The total number of
directors is slightly lower than other listed companies in our coverage. Lotes held 8 board
meetings in 2025 and the actual attendance rate was 95%.
Although the Chairman and CEO are not the same person, they are, in fact, brothers and the co-
founders of Lotes. It gives us a sense that Lotes is still a family enterprise, which is likely a
negative for the board and executive management. That said, the background of independent
directors is diversified with expertise in technology and management. We see it as a positive for
the board's quality.
Capital management 2
Lotes declared a DPS of TWD41.5 in 2025, representing a payout ratio of 50%. Being a leading
connector vendor for applications such as desktops, notebooks and servers, Lotes grew rapidly
along with its key clients (Intel and AMD) on strong technical capabilities and higher growth rate
from the server market. The solid revenue CAGR in the past decade required higher working
capital and continuous capex to expand its capacity, which could also be the culprit for its slightly
lower payout ratio compared with other electronic component makers. Having said that, we think
Lotes’ higher revenue growth should partially offset the drawback of a lower payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1
In 2025, there were no disclosed related-party sales, while purchases from related parties
amounted to TWD4.4m, representing 0.03% of total cost of sales. We see limited risk from
related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2
Lotes established its RBA management system in 2010 and requires its suppliers to meet the
standards of no child labour, no forced labour, treating employees with respect and dignity, and
caring for labourers' safety and health. Lotes hosts a yearly supplier conference to promote the
RBA guidelines to its suppliers. In 2025, 187 suppliers participated in the annual supplier
conference. Lotes also requires its suppliers to sign the RBA-related commitment letters. For
better supply chain management, Lotes conducts yearly audits and regular business reviews
and holds the yearly supplier conference as measures. In 2025, Lotes conducted written
evaluations of 110 suppliers and on-site audits of 61 suppliers. In addition, all 76 newly added
suppliers passed its environmental and social responsibility evaluations. Lotes has required new
suppliers to sign the "Supplier Social and Environmental Responsibility Commitment Letter"
since 2010 to extend social responsibility concepts and requirements to the supply chain.
For "green" purchase management, Lotes has adopted the IECQ QC 080000 hazardous
substance process management system. Lotes asks its suppliers to follow the RoHS, HSF, and
REACH guidelines to avoid using hazardous materials. Lotes also conducts conflict minerals due
diligence and requires suppliers to sign non-conflict mineral declarations and complete surveys
tracing mineral sources to smelters. Overall, Lotes believes it has constructed a comprehensive
system covering system certification (e.g., ISO 9001), product design, supply chain
management, and production techniques to ensure that the entire manufacturing process, from
raw materials to end products, follows green principles. All products from Lotes are now 100%
compliant with RoHS standards.
S Data security 2
Lotes has established a team to implement data security regulations, planning and maintenance.
Moreover, it monitors vulnerabilities in its information systems each year through regular
vulnerability scanning, access control reviews and information security audits. The company
reported no losses resulting from information security incidents in 2025.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기, 물량 감소에도 확인된 이익 방어력 
현대차의 3Q26 매출액은 45조 1,100억원(YoY -3.4%), 영업이익은 2조 6,770억원
(YoY +5.5%), OPM 5.9%(YoY +0.5%p)를 기록했다. 국내 파업 및 영업일수 감소 영
향으로 글로벌 도매 판매량은 92.1만대(YoY -11.3%)까지 감소했으나, HEV 중심의 
고부가 차종 Mix 개선, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비(YoY -1.2조원) 감소 효과
로 수익성은 오히려 개선됐다. 특히 HEV 판매 비중은 18.2%(YoY +2.7%p)까지 상
승했다. 반면 EV 판매는 전년 대비 감소세가 이어지며 지역별 수요 둔화와 경쟁 심
화 영향이 지속됐다. 
연간 눈높이는 낮아졌지만, 믹스 개선은 지속 
현대차는 글로벌 수요 둔화와 생산 차질을 반영해 2026년 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 
기존 415.8만대에서 400만대로, 영업이익률 가이던스를 기존 6.3~7.3%에서 
5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정했다. 다만 북미를 중심으로 HEV 판매가 견조하게 유지되고 
있으며, 하반기 주요 신차 투입과 전동화 라인업 확대를 통해 믹스 개선 기조는 이
어질 전망이다. 중장기적으로는 로보택시 공급 확대와 Atlas 상용화 등 Physical AI 
사업도 계획대로 진행 중으로, 단기 판매 성장 둔화에도 자동차 본업의 수익성 방어
와 신사업 모멘텀은 유효한 것으로 판단한다. 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 460,000원 하향 
글로벌 완성차 업체들의 수익성 부담은 확대되고 있다. 3분기 자동차 부문 영업이익
률은 Mercedes-Benz 2.5%, Stellantis 0.7%, Volvo -5.6%, VW -7.8%를 기록했다. 
유럽 내 BEV 판매 확대에도 중국 전기차 업체들의 공격적인 해외 진출과 가격 경
쟁, 중국 시장 부진이 동시에 나타나면서 전통 OEM의 수익성 압박이 심화되고 있
다. 이러한 환경에서 현대차는 HEV 중심의 유연한 파워트레인 전략과 북미 중심의 
Mix 개선을 통해 5% 후반의 수익성을 유지하고 있다는 점이 상대적으로 부각된다.

Document 301: Anthropic上市路演，CEO称“不在乎什么市场条件”，希望“少承诺、多兑现”

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic上市路演，CEO称“不在乎什么市场条件”，希望“少承诺、多兑现”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 冲刺 IPO 之际， CEO Amodei 路演中刻意低调，强调 “ 少承诺、多兑现 ” ，对上市
时机市场波动表现淡定。⾯对 AI 板块震荡与英伟达⻛波， Amodei 称不特别在意上市⽉份与
市场条件，坦承算⼒投⼊是 “ 最深层的商业难题 ” 。
摘要：
Anthropic 正在冲刺史上最⼤规模 IPO 之⼀，其⾸席执⾏官 Dario Amodei 在上市前路演中展现出
刻意低调的姿态 —— 他表⽰公司希望 " 少承诺、多兑现 " ，并对上市时机的市场波动表现出相当的
淡定。
据《华尔街⽇报》报道， Amodei 于周五在摩根⼤通纽约总部与摩根⼤通 CEO Jamie Dimon 进⾏
了⼀场直播炉边对话，现有和潜在投资者、 Anthropic ⾸席财务官 Krishna Rao 及 Amodei 的妻⼦
均在场。Amodei 在对话中表⽰，公司采取了保守定价策略，他曾多次收到 " 永远不要让公司的
估值超过其真实价值哪怕⼀美元 " 的建议，并认同这⼀思路。
Anthropic 此次 IPO 正值市场剧烈震荡期。 AI 相关股票本⽉初⼤幅下挫，纳斯达克综合指数单⽇

蒸发 2.5 万亿美元；本周英伟达前 CEO Jensen Huang 因涉及私⼈丑闻的离职消息再度引发市场
动荡，英伟达股价承压。⾯对这⼀背景， Amodei 明确表态：
估值较峰值预期⼤幅收缩，定价策略趋于保守
Anthropic ⽬前⽬标估值约为 1.33 万亿美元或更⾼，预计股票将于下周四开始交易，⾼盛和摩根
⼠丹利为本次 IPO 的主承销商，参与承销的投⾏超过 20 家。
这⼀估值与数⽉前市场的预期相⽐已显著回落。华尔街此前曾预计本次 IPO 估值最⾼可达 2 万亿
美元，随后降⾄约 1.5 万亿美元，本⽉初的市场抛售进⼀步压低了预期，部分⼤型投资者也随之
向 Anthropic 施压，要求下调⽬标估值。
Anthropic 本周早些时候披露，计划以每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元的价格发⾏ 8.25 亿股。据知情⼈⼠透露，
鉴于⽬前投资者认购需求强劲，发⾏价区间或发⾏规模有望上调。按当前⽬标区间中值计算，
此次 IPO 募资规模将超过 610 亿美元，另有 260 亿美元来⾃包括⾕歌在内的⼤型投资者的私募配

售。若最终募资超过 750 亿美元， Anthropic 将超越 SpaceX 今年 6 ⽉创下的 IPO 募资纪录。值得注
意的是， Dimon 此前也曾在 SpaceX 上市路演前主持了与⻢斯克的炉边对话。
市场波动加剧，英伟达⾝兼供应商与潜在股东
Anthropic 本次上市⾯临的外部环境不乏挑战。 AI 板块本⽉初⼤幅回调，市场对⾼估值科技股能
否兑现盈利预期的疑虑升温。与此同时，英伟达前 CEO ⻩仁勋宣告离职的消息震惊市场，英伟
达股价随之下跌，进⼀步加剧了投资者的不安情绪。
英伟达与 Anthropic 的关系颇为微妙 ——英伟达既是 Anthropic 的芯⽚供应商，同时也计划与其
他科技巨头⼀道，在此次 IPO 中取得 Anthropic 的⼤额股份。
CEO 坦陈算⼒投⼊是 " 最深层的商业难题 "
" 我们对于在什么⽉份、什么市场条件下上市，并不是特别在意。 "
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

正在加载
在对话中， Amodei 也谈及 Anthropic 更⻓远的发展⽬标，包括利⽤ AI 技术攻克疾病。 " 听起来天
⻢⾏空，听起来很疯狂， " 他说， " 但我真的相信这是可以实现的。 "
在商业层⾯， Amodei 坦承计算资源的投⼊是 " 公司⾯临的最深层的商业难题 " ，巨额算⼒⽀出对
公司财务构成持续压⼒。在估值定价上，他以同样审慎的基调收尾，强调公司的⽬标是 " 少承
诺、多兑现 "—— 这⼀表述既是对投资者预期管理的刻意校准，也折射出当前市场环境下 AI 公司
上市所⾯临的复杂考量。
请发表您的评论
表情 发布评论

Document 331: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research – BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

Document 346: CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận - Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận - Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

1

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

Nguồn: PNJ, KBSV   Nguồn: PNJ, KBSV

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

Document 350: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53.2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities in the coming 6-12 months, 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15-20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid-to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid-cycle bull market. Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

53.2% for our CIO Foundation Balanced portfolio

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex-UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low-teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities. We expect the 
segment to generate 10-15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years, with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High-Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks, MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advances in oncology and innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks 
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. Healthcare and 
consumer staples accounts for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management in a mid-
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and potentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge 
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two, and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets, private equity provides a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, infrastructure offers differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where contracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3-7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell-off caused by AI-
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi-sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010-2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress.

HOUSEHOLD SPENDING
INDICATOR BULLETIN
Spending resilient despite headwinds
4 November 2026
Key points
• Household spending rose 0.6%mth in
September, returning to growth after
August’s brief decline. Q3 spending increased
2.1%qtr, while volumes rose a more subdued

0.7%qtr, pointing to a sharp acceleration
in price growth through the quarter.
• Services spending rebounded and goods
spending remained supportive. Non-
discretionary spending outperformed
discretionary categories, with higher fuel
spending providing significant support.
• In real terms, spending growth over the
quarter was led by transport but with
most other categories also recording

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The monthly household spending indicator rose 0.6%mth in
September (nominal), exceeding both Westpac and market
expectations. We had anticipated a 0.5%mth contraction
(on the basis of a softer read on vehicle sales in the month),

while the market had pencilled in a 0.2%mth rise. Today’s
result instead marks a return to growth following August’s
brief contraction (revised down 0.1ppt to –0.1%mth), with
annual growth strengthening to 6.8%yr. For Q3 as a whole,
nominal spending rose 2.1%qtr, the strongest quarterly
result since the December quarter of 2022. In real terms,
volumes increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, pointing to a
sharp acceleration in price growth through the quarter.

Non-discretionary spending outperforms
By broad category, services spending more than reversed
last month’s decline, rising a solid 1.1%mth (+6.8%yr). Goods
spending held around its recent pace, increasing 0.3%mth
(+6.7%yr). Discretionary spending returned to growth in
September, rising 0.3%mth (+6.9%yr), although this failed to
fully unwind August’s decline.
The relative strength in non-discretionary spending observed
last month continued into September, with the category rising

1.2%mth (+6.5%yr). While the headline result was stronger,
the breakdown aligns with findings from our Westpac–DataX
Card Tracker, and Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel which
show discretionary goods continuing to underperform other
categories (see here and here).
At the detailed level, transport and health led the gains,
both rising 1.5%mth. The increase in transport spending was
largely attributable to higher fuel spending, up 5.4%mth,
reflecting elevated fuel prices, with volumes down 2.2%mth.

The ABS also noted higher spending on vehicle repairs
and maintenance, while domestic air travel increased as
households travelled to attend major sporting events.
Recreation & culture rose 0.8%mth, while miscellaneous
goods & services (+0.6%mth), hotels, cafes & restaurants
(+0.5%mth), and clothing & footwear (+0.1%mth) also
recorded gains.
Partially offsetting these increases, food spending declined
0.1%mth, while furnishings & household equipment fell

0.2%mth, likely reflecting the ongoing weakness in housing
turnover (see here). Alcoholic beverages & tobacco recorded
the largest decline, falling 0.7%mth.
State outcomes were positive across the board in September.
WA led the gains, rising 1.1%mth and reversing last
month’s decline. Victoria also rebounded (+1.0%mth),
while Queensland (+0.6%mth), SA (+0.6%mth), Tasmania
(+0.5%mth) and NSW (+0.3%mth) all recorded increases.

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Volume growth remains firm 
Nominal spending rose a firm 2.1%qtr in Q3, while volumes 
increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, matching the pace recorded 
in Q2. This implies a sharp acceleration in price growth to 
1.4%qtr, from 0.6%qtr in Q2, and marks the strongest quarterly 
increase in prices since the December quarter of 2022. Through 
the year, volume growth strengthened to 3.2%yr, remaining 
well above the 1.8%yr average recorded through 2025.
Transport led gains in volume terms, rising 1.6%qtr, 
likely partly reflecting base effects in air travel following 
disruptions associated with the Middle East conflict in the June 
quarter. Clothing & footwear (+1.2%qtr), health (+1.1%qtr), 
miscellaneous goods & services (+1.1%qtr), food (+0.5%qtr), 
recreation & culture (+0.5%qtr), and furnishings & household 
equipment (+0.1%qtr) also recorded increases. Hotels, cafes 
& restaurants spending declined 0.1%qtr in volume terms, 
while alcoholic beverages & tobacco was the main drag, falling 
1.2%qtr.
Most states recorded real spending growth over the quarter, 
led by WA (+1.4%qtr), Victoria (+0.9%qtr), NSW (+0.5%qtr), 
Queensland (+0.5%qtr) and SA (+0.4%qtr). Tasmania lagged its 
state peers, recording a 0.1%qtr decline.
Conclusion
The household spending indicator provides a useful guide to 
around two-thirds of total consumer expenditure. Importantly, 
it excludes several major categories included in the National 
Accounts measure of real household consumption, including 
rents and utilities.

Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in line with our 
expectations, with Westpac anticipating around a 0.6%qtr rise 
in Q3, and remains consistent with our view on consumption 
ahead of the Q3 National Accounts (due 2 December). 
However, this will likely be the high water mark for spending. 
The renewed deterioration in global energy markets and the 
cumulative effect of four rate increases over the first three 
quarters of the year are likely to weigh more heaveily on 
household spending in Q4.

Looking further ahead, households will continue to face 
a restrictive monetary policy environment, with the RBA 
delivering an additional rate increase at yesterday’s November 
meeting (see here). Combined with persistent consumer 
pessimism, ongoing cost-of-living pressures (see here) and a 
housing downturn impacting household wealth, spending looks 
set to slow further in 2027.

個人消費（小売売上、自動車販売）

マインド・景況感
9月の消費者態度指数（季節調整値）は34.7と3ヵ月連続で上昇した。
9月の景気ウォッチャー調査（現状判断DI、季節調整値）は前月差1.9pt低下の44.1となり、横ばいを示す
50を21ヵ月連続で下回った。秋物需要や連休・インバウンドで人出が増加したものの、物価高による消費
マインドの低下から景況感が悪化した。

住宅投資
8月の住宅着工は、季調・年率14.5万戸と3ヵ月連続で増加し、持ち直しの動きがみられる。前年比では＋
12.3％と大幅に増加。分譲マンション、貸家が増加に寄与した。先行きは、所得環境の改善が下支え要因と

個股與大盤走勢(%)

許哲豪 
jeff.syu@ctbcsis.com

觸控及中小尺寸電子紙 
元太 (8069 TT)

調升評等 
買進(Buy) 
展望 2027年 Signage、 ESL持續帶動整體營運成長  
⚫ 3Q26旺季較淡，模組出貨占比提升，毛利率低於預期  
⚫ 2026全年受消費性電子疲弱再度下修 
⚫ 與台虹合作開發 PTFE，切入高階傳輸材料 
3Q26毛利率低於預期，模組出貨占比提升：公司於 2026年 11月 12日
舉行法說會，3Q26 營收為 99.4億元(QoQ-2.74%，YoY-4.6%)，毛利率 
52.24%(QoQ-6.5ppts，YoY-3.7ppts)，營業利益率 26.6%(QoQ-8.1ppts，
YoY-8.3ppts)，稅後淨利 26.42 億元(QoQ-29.7%，YoY-37.6%)，單季 EPS 
2.3 元。3Q26營收大致符合預期，毛利率低於我們與市場預期，主要因
3Q26 較往年消費性旺季平淡，2026 年消費性電子年增率預期整體下滑
約 15%，且電子紙模組出貨上升，使高毛利電子紙材料出貨占比下滑。
3Q26消費性產品 CE營收占比為 45%，IoT為 55%。 
再度下修 2026年營收成長，展望 2027年仍可成長：前次法說管理層原
預期 2026年營收較前一年成長 10-15%，本次更新展望下修營收年成長
至約 9%%，主要受消費性產品預期全年下滑約 15%，而 IoT較為強勁，
成長 35%以上，彌補消費性產品營收衰退。管理層展望 2027年營運雖未
給出具體指引，仍預期營收持續成長，主要由 Signage、ESL持續成長帶
動，其中 Signage較不受記憶體價格影響，並隨著 H5、H6產能及良率提
升，成本競爭力提升，使出貨量擴大，預估毛利率隨新產線良率改善仍
有成長空間。 
切入 AI高階材料，有望 2028量產貢獻營收：元太與台虹合作開發 AI
高速傳輸用 PTFE 材料，應用於資料中心、衛星、車用自駕及機器人，
並持有台虹股權約 15.7%。公司先前在觀音購買土地並興建新廠，也與
華紙購入既有廠房與土地，將整合兩者生產基地，並由台虹提供技術與
材料，元太提供產線及代工。預期 PTFE代工於 2027年小量貢獻，2028
年顯著放量。 
公司雖持續受記憶體成本影響消費性產品營收而下修 2026 年營運展
望，但公司長期營運展望 ESL以及數位看板成長態勢不變，且積極布局
多項非消費性領域大尺寸電子紙應用(交通產業、機器人等)，且切入 PTFE
材料新領域，有望帶來新成長動能。我們預估 2026、2027年 EPS分別
為為 10.89、11.59元，我們基於 2027年預估 EPS x 18倍本益比，目標
價為 209元。公司評價已交易至過往區間極下緣位階，我們調升至買進
之投資評等建議。風險因子：消費性需求不如預期、大尺寸電子紙產能
爬坡不如預期。
